alt=\"codecov\" data-canonical-src=\"https://codecov.io/gh/Tonejs/Tone.js/branch/dev/graph/badge.svg\" style=\"max-width: 100%;\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTone.js is a Web Audio framework for creating interactive music in the browser. The architecture of Tone.js aims to be familiar to both musicians and audio programmers creating web-based audio applications. On the high-level, Tone offers common DAW (digital audio workstation) features like a global transport for synchronizing and scheduling events as well as prebuilt synths and effects. Additionally, Tone provides high-performance building blocks to create your own synthesizers, effects, and complex control signals.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://tonejs.github.io/docs/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eAPI\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://tonejs.github.io/examples/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eExamples\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch1 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eInstallation\u003c/h1\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-installation\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Installation\" href=\"#installation\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThere are two ways to incorporate Tone.js into a project. everything. have a few built in methods for creating automation curves.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor example, the \u003ccode\u003efrequency\u003c/code\u003e parameter on class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Tone.js</h1><a id="user-content-tonejs" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Tone.js" href="#tonejs"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://codecov.io/gh/Tonejs/Tone.js" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/e9e6e02db0a1bc839e221b98c4a413370645bcfbaaa7f70072d411e1a9278f63/68747470733a2f2f636f6465636f762e696f2f67682f546f6e656a732f546f6e652e6a732f6272616e63682f6465762f67726170682f62616467652e737667" alt="codecov" data-canonical-src="https://codecov.io/gh/Tonejs/Tone.js/branch/dev/graph/badge.svg" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto">Tone.js is a Web Audio framework for creating interactive music in the browser. The architecture of Tone.js aims to be familiar to both musicians and audio programmers creating web-based audio applications. On the high-level, Tone offers common DAW (digital audio workstation) features like a global transport for synchronizing and scheduling events as well as prebuilt synths and effects. Additionally, Tone provides high-performance building blocks to create your own synthesizers, effects, and complex control signals.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://tonejs.github.io/docs/" rel="nofollow">API</a></li>
<li><a href="https://tonejs.github.io/examples/" rel="nofollow">Examples</a></li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installation</h1><a id="user-content-installation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installation" href="#installation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">There are two ways to incorporate Tone.js into a project. First, it can be installed locally into a project using <code>npm</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npm install tone      // Install the latest stable version
npm install tone@next // Or, alternatively, use the 'next' version"><pre>npm install tone      // Install the latest stable version
npm install tone@next // Or, alternatively, use the <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>next<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> version</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Add Tone.js to a project using the JavaScript <code>import</code> syntax:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import * as Tone from &quot;tone&quot;;"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-c1">*</span> <span class="pl-k">as</span> <span class="pl-v">Tone</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">"tone"</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Tone.js is also hosted at unpkg.com. It can be added directly within an HTML document, as long as it precedes any project scripts. <a href="https://github.com/Tonejs/Tone.js/blob/master/examples/simpleHtml.html">See the example here</a> for more details.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;script src=&quot;http://unpkg.com/tone&quot;&gt;&lt;/script&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">script</span> <span class="pl-c1">src</span>="<span class="pl-s">http://unpkg.com/tone</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">script</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Hello Tone</h1><a id="user-content-hello-tone" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Hello Tone" href="#hello-tone"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="//create a synth and connect it to the main output (your speakers)
const synth = new Tone.Synth().toDestination();

//play a middle 'C' for the duration of an 8th note

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Tone.Synth</h2><a id="user-content-tonesynth" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Tone.Synth" href="#tonesynth"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>Tone.Synth</code> is a basic synthesizer with a single oscillator and an ADSR envelope.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">triggerAttack / triggerRelease</h3><a id="user-content-triggerattack--triggerrelease" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: triggerAttack / triggerRelease" href="#triggerattack--triggerrelease"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>triggerAttack</code> starts the note (the amplitude is rising), and <code>triggerRelease</code> is when the amplitude is going back to 0 (i.e. <strong>note off</strong>).</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="const synth = new Tone.Synth().toDestination();
const now = Tone.now();
// trigger the attack immediately
synth.triggerAttack(&quot;C4&quot;, now);
// wait one second before triggering the release
synth.triggerRelease(now + 1);"><pre><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">synth</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">Synth</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">toDestination</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">now</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">now</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-c">// trigger the attack immediately</span>
<span class="pl-s1">synth</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">triggerAttack</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"C4"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">now</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-c">// wait one second before triggering the release</span>
<span class="pl-s1">synth</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">triggerRelease</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">now</span> <span class="pl-c1">+</span> <span class="pl-c1">1</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">triggerAttackRelease</h3><a id="user-content-triggerattackrelease" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: triggerAttackRelease" href="#triggerattackrelease"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>triggerAttackRelease</code> is a combination of <code>triggerAttack</code> and <code>triggerRelease</code></p>
<p dir="auto">The first argument to the note which can either be a frequency in hertz (like <code>440</code>) or as "pitch-octave" notation (like <code>"D#2"</code>).</p>
<p dir="auto">The second argument is the duration that the note is held. This value can either be in seconds, or as a <a href="https://github.com/Tonejs/Tone.js/wiki/Time">tempo-relative value</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">The third (optional) argument of <code>triggerAttackRelease</code> is <em>when</em> along the AudioContext time the note should play. It can be used to schedule events in the future.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="const synth = new Tone.Synth().toDestination();
const now = Tone.now();
synth.triggerAttackRelease(&quot;C4&quot;, &quot;8n&quot;, now);
synth.triggerAttackRelease(&quot;E4&quot;, &quot;8n&quot;, now + 0.5);
synth.triggerAttackRelease(&quot;G4&quot;, &quot;8n&quot;, now + 1);"><pre><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">synth</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">Synth</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">toDestination</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">now</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">now</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">synth</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">triggerAttackRelease</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"C4"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"8n"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">now</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">synth</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">triggerAttackRelease</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"E4"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"8n"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">now</span> <span class="pl-c1">+</span> <span class="pl-c1">0.5</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">synth</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">triggerAttackRelease</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"G4"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"8n"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">now</span> <span class="pl-c1">+</span> <span class="pl-c1">1</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Time</h2><a id="user-content-time" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Time" href="#time"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Web Audio has advanced, sample accurate scheduling capabilities. The AudioContext time is what the Web Audio API uses to schedule events, starts at 0 when the page loads and counts up in <strong>seconds</strong>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>Tone.now()</code> gets the current time of the AudioContext.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="setInterval(() =&gt; console.log(Tone.now()), 100);"><pre><span class="pl-en">setInterval</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">now</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">100</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Tone.js abstracts away the AudioContext time. Instead of defining all values in seconds, any method which takes time as an argument can accept a number or a string. For example <code>"4n"</code> is a quarter-note, <code>"8t"</code> is an eighth-note triplet, and <code>"1m"</code> is one measure.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/Tonejs/Tone.js/wiki/Time">Read about Time encodings</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Starting Audio</h1><a id="user-content-starting-audio" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Starting Audio" href="#starting-audio"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>IMPORTANT</strong>: Browsers will not play <em>any</em> audio until a user clicks something (like a play button). Run your Tone.js code only after calling <code>Tone.start()</code> from a event listener which is triggered by a user action such as "click" or "keydown".</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>Tone.start()</code> returns a promise, the audio will be ready only after that promise is resolved. Scheduling or playing audio before the AudioContext is running will result in silence or incorrect scheduling.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="//attach a click listener to a play button
document.querySelector(&quot;button&quot;)?.addEventListener(&quot;click&quot;, async () =&gt; {
	await Tone.start();
	console.log(&quot;audio is ready&quot;);
});"><pre><span class="pl-c">//attach a click listener to a play button</span>
<span class="pl-smi">document</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">querySelector</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"button"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">?.</span><span class="pl-en">addEventListener</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"click"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
	<span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">start</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
	<span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"audio is ready"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Scheduling</h1><a id="user-content-scheduling" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Scheduling" href="#scheduling"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Transport</h2><a id="user-content-transport" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Transport" href="#transport"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>Tone.getTransport()</code> returns the main timekeeper. Unlike the AudioContext clock, it can be started, stopped, looped and adjusted on the fly. You can think of it like the arrangement view in a Digital Audio Workstation.</p>
<p dir="auto">Multiple events and parts can be arranged and synchronized along the Transport. <code>Tone.Loop</code> is a simple way to create a looped callback that can be scheduled to start and stop.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// create two monophonic synths
const synthA = new Tone.FMSynth().toDestination();
const synthB = new Tone.AMSynth().toDestination();
//play a note every quarter-note
const loopA = new Tone.Loop((time) =&gt; {
	synthA.triggerAttackRelease(&quot;C2&quot;, &quot;8n&quot;, time);
}, &quot;4n&quot;).start(0);
//play another note every off quarter-note, by starting it &quot;8n&quot;
const loopB = new Tone.Loop((time) =&gt; {
	synthB.triggerAttackRelease(&quot;C4&quot;, &quot;8n&quot;, time);
}, &quot;4n&quot;).start(&quot;8n&quot;);
// all loops start when the Transport is started
Tone.getTransport().start();
// ramp up to 800 bpm over 10 seconds
Tone.getTransport().bpm.rampTo(800, 10);"><pre><span class="pl-c">// create two monophonic synths</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">synthA</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">FMSynth</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">toDestination</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">synthB</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">AMSynth</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">toDestination</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-c">//play a note every quarter-note</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">loopA</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">Loop</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">time</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
	<span class="pl-s1">synthA</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">triggerAttackRelease</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"C2"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"8n"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">time</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"4n"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">start</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-c1">0</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-c">//play another note every off quarter-note, by starting it "8n"</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">loopB</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">Loop</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">time</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
	<span class="pl-s1">synthB</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">triggerAttackRelease</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"C4"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"8n"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">time</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"4n"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">start</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"8n"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-c">// all loops start when the Transport is started</span>
<span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">getTransport</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">start</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-c">// ramp up to 800 bpm over 10 seconds</span>
<span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">getTransport</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">bpm</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">rampTo</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-c1">800</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">10</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Since Javascript callbacks are <strong>not precisely timed</strong>, the sample-accurate time of the event is passed into the callback function. <strong>Use this time value to schedule the events</strong>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Instruments</h1><a id="user-content-instruments" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Instruments" href="#instruments"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">There are numerous synths to choose from including <code>Tone.FMSynth</code>, <code>Tone.AMSynth</code> and <code>Tone.NoiseSynth</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">All of these instruments are <strong>monophonic</strong> (single voice) which means that they can only play one note at a time.</p>
<p dir="auto">To create a <strong>polyphonic</strong> synthesizer, use <code>Tone.PolySynth</code>, which accepts a monophonic synth as its first parameter and automatically handles the note allocation so you can pass in multiple notes. The API is similar to the monophonic synths, except <code>triggerRelease</code> must be given a note or array of notes.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="const synth = new Tone.PolySynth(Tone.Synth).toDestination();
const now = Tone.now();
synth.triggerAttack(&quot;D4&quot;, now);
synth.triggerAttack(&quot;F4&quot;, now + 0.5);
synth.triggerAttack(&quot;A4&quot;, now + 1);
synth.triggerAttack(&quot;C5&quot;, now + 1.5);
synth.triggerAttack(&quot;E5&quot;, now + 2);
synth.triggerRelease([&quot;D4&quot;, &quot;F4&quot;, &quot;A4&quot;, &quot;C5&quot;, &quot;E5&quot;], now + 4);"><pre><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">synth</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">PolySynth</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">Synth</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">toDestination</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">now</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">now</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">synth</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">triggerAttack</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"D4"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">now</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">synth</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">triggerAttack</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"F4"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">now</span> <span class="pl-c1">+</span> <span class="pl-c1">0.5</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">synth</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">triggerAttack</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"A4"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">now</span> <span class="pl-c1">+</span> <span class="pl-c1">1</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">synth</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">triggerAttack</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"C5"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">now</span> <span class="pl-c1">+</span> <span class="pl-c1">1.5</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">synth</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">triggerAttack</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"E5"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">now</span> <span class="pl-c1">+</span> <span class="pl-c1">2</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">synth</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">triggerRelease</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-s">"D4"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"F4"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"A4"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"C5"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"E5"</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">now</span> <span class="pl-c1">+</span> <span class="pl-c1">4</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Samples</h1><a id="user-content-samples" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Samples" href="#samples"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Sound generation is not limited to synthesized sounds. You can also load a sample and play that back in a number of ways. <code>Tone.Player</code> is one way to load and play back an audio file.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="const player = new Tone.Player(
	&quot;https://tonejs.github.io/audio/berklee/gong_1.mp3&quot;
).toDestination();
Tone.loaded().then(() =&gt; {
	player.start();
});"><pre><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">player</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">Player</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span>
	<span class="pl-s">"https://tonejs.github.io/audio/berklee/gong_1.mp3"</span>
<span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">toDestination</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">loaded</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">then</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
	<span class="pl-s1">player</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">start</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>Tone.loaded()</code> returns a promise which resolves when <em>all</em> audio files are loaded. It's a helpful shorthand instead of waiting on each individual audio buffer's <code>onload</code> event to resolve.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Tone.Sampler</h2><a id="user-content-tonesampler" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Tone.Sampler" href="#tonesampler"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Multiple samples can also be combined into an instrument. If you have audio files organized by note, <code>Tone.Sampler</code> will pitch shift the samples to fill in gaps between notes. So for example, if you only have every 3rd note on a piano sampled, you could turn that into a full piano sample.</p>
<p dir="auto">Unlike the other synths, Tone.Sampler is polyphonic so doesn't need to be passed into Tone.PolySynth</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="const sampler = new Tone.Sampler({
	urls: {
		C4: &quot;C4.mp3&quot;,
		&quot;D#4&quot;: &quot;Ds4.mp3&quot;,
		&quot;F#4&quot;: &quot;Fs4.mp3&quot;,
		A4: &quot;A4.mp3&quot;,
	},
	release: 1,
	baseUrl: &quot;https://tonejs.github.io/audio/salamander/&quot;,
}).toDestination();

Tone.loaded().then(() =&gt; {
	sampler.triggerAttackRelease([&quot;Eb4&quot;, &quot;G4&quot;, &quot;Bb4&quot;], 4);
});"><pre><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">sampler</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">Sampler</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
	<span class="pl-c1">urls</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
		<span class="pl-c1">C4</span>: <span class="pl-s">"C4.mp3"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
		<span class="pl-s">"D#4"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"Ds4.mp3"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>

		<span class="pl-c1">A4</span>: <span class="pl-s">"A4.mp3"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
	<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
	<span class="pl-c1">release</span>: <span class="pl-c1">1</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
	<span class="pl-c1">baseUrl</span>: <span class="pl-s">"https://tonejs.github.io/audio/salamander/"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">toDestination</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">loaded</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">then</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
	<span class="pl-s1">sampler</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">triggerAttackRelease</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-s">"Eb4"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"G4"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"Bb4"</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">4</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Effects</h1><a id="user-content-effects" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Effects" href="#effects"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">In the above examples, the sources were always connected directly to the <code>Destination</code>, but the output of the synth could also be routed through one (or more) effects before going to the speakers.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="const player = new Tone.Player({
	url: &quot;https://tonejs.github.io/audio/berklee/gurgling_theremin_1.mp3&quot;,
	loop: true,
	autostart: true,
});
//create a distortion effect
const distortion = new Tone.Distortion(0.4).toDestination();
//connect a player to the distortion
player.connect(distortion);"><pre><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">player</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">Player</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
	<span class="pl-c1">url</span>: <span class="pl-s">"https://tonejs.github.io/audio/berklee/gurgling_theremin_1.mp3"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
	<span class="pl-c1">loop</span>: <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
	<span class="pl-c1">autostart</span>: <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-c">//create a distortion effect</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">distortion</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">Distortion</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-c1">0.4</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">toDestination</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-c">//connect a player to the distortion</span>
<span class="pl-s1">player</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">connect</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">distortion</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The connection routing is flexible, connections can run serially or in parallel.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="const player = new Tone.Player({
	url: &quot;https://tonejs.github.io/audio/drum-samples/loops/ominous.mp3&quot;,
	autostart: true,
});
const filter = new Tone.Filter(400, &quot;lowpass&quot;).toDestination();
const feedbackDelay = new Tone.FeedbackDelay(0.125, 0.5).toDestination();

// connect the player to the feedback delay and filter in parallel
player.connect(filter);
player.connect(feedbackDelay);"><pre><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">player</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">Player</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
	<span class="pl-c1">url</span>: <span class="pl-s">"https://tonejs.github.io/audio/drum-samples/loops/ominous.mp3"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
	<span class="pl-c1">autostart</span>: <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">filter</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">Filter</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-c1">400</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"lowpass"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">toDestination</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">feedbackDelay</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">FeedbackDelay</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-c1">0.125</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">0.5</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">toDestination</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// connect the player to the feedback delay and filter in parallel</span>
<span class="pl-s1">player</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">connect</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">filter</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">player</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">connect</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">feedbackDelay</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Multiple nodes can be connected to the same input enabling sources to share effects. <code>Tone.Gain</code> is useful utility node for creating complex routing.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Signals</h1><a id="user-content-signals" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Signals" href="#signals"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Like the underlying Web Audio API, Tone.js is built with audio-rate signal control over nearly everything. This is a powerful feature which allows for sample-accurate synchronization and scheduling of parameters.</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>Signal</code> properties have a few built in methods for creating automation curves.</p>
<p dir="auto">For example, the <code>frequency</code> parameter on <code>Oscillator</code> is a Signal so you can create a smooth ramp from one frequency to another.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="const osc = new Tone.Oscillator().toDestination();
// start at &quot;C4&quot;
osc.frequency.value = &quot;C4&quot;;
// ramp to &quot;C2&quot; over 2 seconds
osc.frequency.rampTo(&quot;C2&quot;, 2);
// start the oscillator for 2 seconds
osc.start().stop(&quot;+3&quot;);"><pre><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">osc</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Tone</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">Oscillator</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">toDestination</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-c">// start at "C4"</span>
<span class="pl-s1">osc</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">frequency</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">value</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">"C4"</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-c">// ramp to "C2" over 2 seconds</span>
<span class="pl-s1">osc</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">frequency</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">rampTo</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"C2"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">2</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-c">// start the oscillator for 2 seconds</span>
<span class="pl-s1">osc</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">start</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">stop</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"+3"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">AudioContext</h1><a id="user-content-audiocontext" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: AudioContext" href="#audiocontext"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Tone.js creates an AudioContext when it loads and shims it for maximum browser compatibility using <a href="https://github.com/chrisguttandin/standardized-audio-context">standardized-audio-context</a>. The AudioContext can be accessed at <code>Tone.getContext</code>. Or set your own AudioContext using <code>Tone.setContext(audioContext)</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">MIDI</h1><a id="user-content-midi" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: MIDI" href="#midi"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To use MIDI files, you'll first need to convert them into a JSON format which Tone.js can understand using <a href="https://tonejs.github.io/Midi/" rel="nofollow">Midi</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Performance</h1><a id="user-content-performance" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Performance" href="#performance"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Tone.js makes extensive use of the native Web Audio Nodes such as the GainNode and WaveShaperNode for all signal processing, which enables Tone.js to work well on both desktop and mobile browsers.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://github.com/Tonejs/Tone.js/wiki/Performance">This wiki</a> article has some suggestions related to performance for best practices.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Testing</h1><a id="user-content-testing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Testing" href="#testing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Tone.js runs an extensive test suite using <a href="https://mochajs.org/" rel="nofollow">mocha</a> and <a href="http://chaijs.com/" rel="nofollow">chai</a> with nearly 100% coverage. Passing builds on the 'dev' branch are published on npm as <code>tone@next</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Contributing</h1><a id="user-content-contributing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Contributing" href="#contributing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">There are many ways to contribute to Tone.js. Check out <a href="https://github.com/Tonejs/Tone.js/wiki/Contributing">this wiki</a> if you're interested.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">References and Inspiration</h1><a id="user-content-references-and-inspiration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: References and Inspiration" href="#references-and-inspiration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
